Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v0.21.43 Stable2026-10-30T11:36:30Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.43

0.21.43 is a small patch release that corrects native process identity recovery guidance in omx doctor.

Highlights

  • PROCESS IDENTITY RECOVERY GUIDANCE (#3971): When the native process identity provider is unsupported or unavailable, omx doctor recommends omx update to hydrate the release-asset runtime cache. The guidance retains that session pointer recovery works without the native runtime.

Fixes and compatibility

  • Removed omx setup from the Process identity cache-repair hint; setup does not repair the native runtime cache. Other failure cases retain their existing reinstall/update guidance.
  • No breaking API changes. Package, npm lockfile, Cargo workspace/lockfile, and plugin metadata were already synchronized to 0.21.43 by #3970; the release branch does not double-bump them.

Known issues and exclusions

  • The separate provider-unavailable report in #3968 (reporter Kevin-wenyu) remains unconfirmed; evidence was requested. This release corrects the remediation text and does not claim to fix the underlying reported provider failure.
  • #3898 remains held for the owner design decision. Related PRs #3939, #3900, #3907, #3915, and #3919 remain excluded.
  • #3965 is not included; changes were requested and the work is split pending. No other user-visible changes in the compare range are excluded.

v0.21.42 Stable2026-10-30T06:48:57Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.42

0.21.42 is a patch release that makes Team and tmux status inspection read-only and improves native Codex goal reconciliation.

Highlights

  • READ-ONLY TEAM AND TMUX STATUS (#3963): omx team status no longer runs monitor maintenance, writes state, or recovers claims, so inspection remains safe with a read-only OMX root. An omitted team name resolves only from a unique current-leader match. omx tmux-hook status reports missing configuration without initializing it.
  • NATIVE CODEX GOAL RECONCILIATION (#3964): Normalize structured and serialized get_goal response envelopes, distinguish a missing native goal tool from database/schema/context errors, and allow truthful non-terminal blocked checkpoints while verification is unavailable without fabricating goal completion.

Fixes and compatibility

  • Detect same-inode history content changes even when size and modification time are restored; do not publish the altered history artifact (#3959).
  • Stabilize detached-launch and Windows platform test fixtures, including per-fixture sidecar isolation (#3963).
  • No breaking API changes. Package, npm lockfile, Cargo workspace/lockfile, and plugin metadata were already synchronized to 0.21.42 by #3962; the release does not double-bump them.

Known issues and exclusions

  • #3898 remains held for the owner design decision; related PRs #3939, #3900, #3907, #3915, and #3919 remain excluded.
  • #3965 is not included; its changes are requested and the work is split pending.
  • No other user-facing changes in the compare range are excluded.

v0.21.41 Stable2026-10-29T19:25:18Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.41

0.21.41 is a patch release that adds configurable custom quality gates to the deep-interview, Ralplan, and code-review stages.

Highlights

  • CONFIGURABLE CUSTOM QUALITY GATES (#3956; requested in #3955 by @iqdoctor): Add optional skill-based reviewers through .omx-config.json qualityGates entries for deep-interview, ralplan, and code-review. String entries default to blocking; objects may set mode (blocking or advisory) and an optional model. Gates supplement built-in review; advisory verdicts are retained without blocking, and blocking Ralplan reject/iterate verdicts feed the revise loop. An unavailable gate is surfaced as unavailable rather than represented as an approval. omx quality-gates list --stage ... reports normalized stage configuration.

Fixes and compatibility

  • No breaking API changes.
  • Model-selection documentation now matches the implementation (#3951).
  • Detect same-inode history-file content changes even when a writer restores the original size and modification time; the altered history artifact is not published (#3958).
  • The development package, npm lockfile, Cargo workspace/lockfile, and plugin metadata are synchronized at 0.21.41 by the release-train changes (#3953).

Known issues and exclusions

  • #3898 remains held for the owner design decision. Related PRs #3939, #3900, #3907, #3915, and #3919 remain excluded.
  • No other user-facing changes in the compare range are excluded.

Merged PR inventory

PR Author Title Impact

#3952 @Yeachan-Heo docs: record v0.21.40 publication evidence Preserve previous-release publication evidence on dev
#3953 @Yeachan-Heo chore: back-merge main (v0.21.40) to dev and bump to 0.21.41 Synchronize dev with shipped main and set the next development base version
#3954 @Yeachan-Heo back-merge: main to dev for v0.21.40 Internal follow-up merge aligning main and dev
#3956 @Yeachan-Heo feat(#3955): Add configurable custom quality gates via .omx-config Add validated stage-specific gates, listing CLI, Ralplan integration, and workflow guidance
#3958 @Yeachan-Heo fix(history): detect same-mtime content mutations during publication Include file change time in history version checks; prevent publishing mutated history when size and mtime are restored

Validation

  • Pinned dev candidate 4a0f645b898e74d381ee7d4da1ced0bf26280896 passed CI run 40910124408: 24 jobs succeeded, 1 publish job was skipped, 0 failed or pending, and all 25 job names were unique. opendatalab/MinerU ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
mineru-4.1.3-releasedMinerU 4.1.3 Stable2026-10-30T10:43:37Z

中文

  • Router 动态节点管理：支持运行时注册、查看和注销远程节点，通过独立管理密钥控制访问，无需重启即可调整节点池。
  • 节点平滑退出：注销节点后停止分配新任务，等待已有任务、请求、下载及结果保留引用释放后再移除。
  • API 过期时间精度：内部保留亚秒级时间精度，避免短保留期因整秒取整而提前失效；公开时间戳保持整数格式。
  • 请求与运行时稳定性：修复重复文件传输可能触发的连接重置，改善反向代理路径前缀与 Unicode URL 兼容性，并优化空闲模型运行时的退出清理。
  • 文档与集成示例：整理用户文档和历史资料，增加旧链接重定向与站点检查，补充 Tasks API、Router 管理、curl 和 n8n 示例。

English

  • Dynamic Router upstreams: Register, inspect, and deregister remote workers at runtime using an independent management key.
  • Graceful worker draining: Deregistered workers stop receiving new tasks and remain available until existing tasks, requests, downloads, and retained resource references are released.
  • Precise API expiration: Preserve subsecond precision internally to prevent short retention periods from expiring early, while keeping public timestamps as integers.
  • Request and runtime stability: Fix connection resets during duplicate file transfers, improve reverse-proxy path prefix and Unicode URL handling, and refine idle model runtime cleanup.
  • Documentation and integration examples: Reorganize current and historical documentation, add redirects and site checks, and expand Tasks API, Router management, curl, and n8n examples.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/compare/mineru-4.1.2-released...mineru-4.1.3-released

mineru-4.1.2-releasedMinerU 4.1.2 Stable2026-10-29T17:11:38Z

中文

  • 硬件诊断效率与输出：mineru-kit hardware info 并行执行隔离的运行时探测，文本报告按固定顺序逐节输出并刷新；统一容量显示，精简设备与运行时摘要，JSON 保留完整诊断信息并补充 ONNX 只读候选设备及原因。
  • CLI 帮助按需加载：根帮助、硬件帮助和版本查询不再初始化运行环境或读取用户配置，仅在实际执行命令时加载对应入口。
  • 问题反馈表单：提供独立的中英文表单，精简必填信息，解析档位默认选中“未知”；发布后自动同步最近五个稳定版本供选择。
  • CI 与发布流程：问题表单变更使用轻量检查，发布重跑仍执行完整测试，避免被后续分支提交取消。

English

  • Hardware diagnostics and output: mineru-kit hardware info runs isolated runtime probes concurrently and flushes text sections in a fixed order as they become available. Capacity formatting and device summaries are clearer, while JSON retains full diagnostics and adds the read-only ONNX device candidate and selection reason.
  • On-demand CLI help: Root help, hardware help, and version queries no longer initialize the runtime or read user configuration. Command entry points load only when executed.
  • Issue reporting: Separate English and Chinese forms require less information, default the parsing tier to “Unknown,” and automatically offer the five most recent stable versions after each release.
  • CI and release workflows: Issue-form changes use lightweight checks, while release reruns retain the full test suite and are protected from cancellation by later branch pushes.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/compare/mineru-4.1.1-released...mineru-4.1.2-released

mineru-4.1.1-releasedMinerU 4.1.1 Stable2026-10-29T15:31:40Z

中文

  • 简化自动档位推荐：移除推荐流程中的显存检测与容量门槛，直接复用小模型后端实际选择的 CPU/GPU 设备。选中 GPU 时开放全部四档，默认使用 Standard，Advanced 需要显式指定；选中 CPU 时，按进程可用逻辑核数推荐：不足 4 核仅开放 Flash，4–11 核开放到 Basic，至少 12 核开放到 Standard。
  • 统一 llama.cpp 运行策略：Linux 和 Windows 自动模式复用小模型后端的 CPU/GPU 选择，具体显卡由 llama.cpp 选择。这些平台上的 GPU 主模型默认使用 4 个生成线程，保留用户显式设置。
  • 改善 PDF 文本清理：在后置 OCR 和失败恢复完成后，将原生正文中残留的 Unicode 私用区字符替换为空格，减少异常字体映射造成的乱码，覆盖基本和补充私用区。

English

  • Simplified automatic tier recommendations: Remove GPU memory detection and capacity thresholds from tier recommendations, reusing the CPU/GPU device actually selected by the small-model backend. GPU selection enables all four tiers, with Standard as the default and Advanced available by explicit request. CPU selection uses the logical cores available to the process: fewer than 4 enables Flash only, 4–11 enables up to Basic, and 12 or more enables up to Standard.
  • Unified llama.cpp runtime policy: Automatic mode on Linux and Windows reuses the small-model backend's CPU/GPU selection and lets llama.cpp choose the specific GPU. On these platforms, GPU models default to 4 generation threads while preserving explicit user settings.
  • Improved PDF text cleanup: Replace residual Unicode private-use characters in native body text with spaces after post-OCR processing and fallback restoration, reducing garbled text caused by unusual font mappings. Both basic and supplementary private-use areas are covered.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/compare/mineru-4.1.0-released...mineru-4.1.1-released

anomalyco/opencode ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v2.2.3 Stable2026-10-30T09:55:45Z

Highlights

  • Use Claude Code in OpenCode: Install Claude Code, run claude auth login, then select Claude Agent in /connect to use your Claude Code login and models. (#58782)
  • Faster Long Sessions in Desktop: Streaming replies and long conversations now do much less rendering work. (#60489, #60521, #60513, #60481)
  • Desktop Stability Fixes: Fixes side-panel tabs, repeated "Session not found" toasts, and Open file over HTTP. (#60430, #60436, #60459, #60462)

Breaking changes

TUI plugins require OpenTUI 0.8

@opencode/plugin now requires @opentui/core and @opentui/solid 0.8.0 or later. In OpenTUI 0.8, a ScrollBox's wrapper, viewport, content, and scrollbars are facade objects instead of renderables, and a custom RenderContext must implement clock and onAnimationTick. See the OpenTUI 0.8.0 release notes. (#60385)

Managed policy: integration.use covers integrations only

integration.use now controls which integration accounts can be used, and the new mcp.use, skill.use, and plugin.use actions control MCP servers, skills, and plugins. Rules with the old mcp:, skill:, and plugin: resource prefixes are converted automatically, but a bare integration.use rule no longer applies to MCP servers, skills, or plugins. (#60315)

Core

Improvements

  • Added the built-in Claude Agent provider. Install Claude Code, run claude auth login, and select Claude Agent in /connect; OPENCODE_CLAUDE_PATH can override the Claude executable. Thanks @Tarquinen. (#58782)
  • Added Code Mode JavaScript checks to experimental schedules, including statically authorized tool calls, persistent state, and custom wake messages. (#60140)
  • Added separate managed-policy actions for connecting to integrations and using integrations, MCP servers, skills, and plugins. (#60315)

Bug fixes

  • Preserved plugin-provided model variants and delayed dynamic provider fallback until external SDK hooks have run. (#60097, #60380)
  • Preserved permission reasons and feedback in MCP failures, and made Code Mode failures match direct tool-call failures. (#60102, #60109)
  • Fixed invalid Code Mode schedule checks so creation fails immediately and existing invalid checks pause without repeated execution. (#60349)
  • Removed a session’s persistent terminals when the session is deleted. (#60322)
  • Kept nested tool progress from replacing the parent execute call rows. (#60480)

TUI

Improvements

  • Added lazy loading for stored image references when experimental.media_blob is enabled. Images are downloaded only for previews, while undo, revert, fork, and prompt recall restore referenced attachments safely. (#60116)
  • Added a distinct marker for the selected composer suggestion in the new designs. (#60393)

Bug fixes

  • Fixed PHP syntax highlighting for code blocks without an opening <?php tag. (#60359)
  • Preserved # in file-search paths unless it introduces a valid line range. (#60336)
  • Kept the configured default cursor style after config reloads. (#60302)
  • Matched the search cursor to the highlighted row when a selection dialog has no query. (#60310)

Desktop

Improvements

  • Added a Desktop spellcheck toggle under Settings → General and system-language dictionary selection on Windows and Linux. (#59129)
  • Added PageUp and PageDown timeline paging from the composer without moving the draft cursor or focus. (#59426)
  • Added independently configurable question notifications and sounds under Settings → Notifications → Questions. Thanks @YuriNek0. (#60358)
  • Added separate project and worktree actions to the new-session control. (#60486)
  • Added one-click version copying in Settings → About. (#52356)
  • Grouped added projects in workspace settings. (#60221)
  • Made question-answer text selectable. (#60372)
  • Adapted the session header to narrow timeline widths. (#59512)

Bug fixes

  • Side-panel tabs close again and New tab launchers respond to clicks, fixing a 2.2.2 regression. (#60430, #60436)
  • Open file in the side panel works again in the web app over HTTP. (#60462)
  • Kept question options and submit controls reachable on mobile. (#60335)
  • Preserved simultaneous request notifications and inbox scroll position when opening sessions. (#60378, #60356)
  • Fixed consecutive dropped files so every file mention is inserted into the composer. (#60463)
  • Kept the current @ and command-menu selection stable while results update under a stationary pointer. (#59434, #60368)
  • Fixed ⌘W side-panel closing and focus restoration when no closable tabs remain. (#59002, #60435)
  • Kept the mobile session drawer open while a session loads. (#59427)
  • Restored maximized windows on their saved display and retained renderer permissions across shared-session windows. (#60482, #60458)
  • Fixed timeline growth, zoom-end scrolling, highlighting, and DOM-observer performance issues in long sessions. (#60489, #60481, #60488, #60513, #60521)
  • Kept models with unknown release dates visible. Thanks @joe-ieta. (#60496)
  • Fixed reasoning-capability detection for models that expose reasoning through variants or compatibility metadata. (#60457)
  • Evicted deleted sessions after a 404 instead of retrying or showing repeated errors. (#60459)
  • Fixed the macOS application icon and added the required folder-access description to the app bundle. (#60020, #60503)

SDK

HyperFrames v0.8.221

Released on 2026-10-30.

Snapshots of video projects take less time because each clip is read once for all the times you ask for. Preview now stops at once when you press Ctrl+C.

Fixes

  • CLI: Snapshot probes each video clip once for all the times it is asked for (b3c47a36a, #6798).
  • CLI: Preview stops at once on Ctrl+C after Studio was open (3e795ece0, #6782).
  • CLI: Add takes several names in one call, and adopted audio is labelled voice or sfx (7f7a4af7c, #6791).
  • CLI: Check no longer flags a hidden scene child or a small animated sprite (8c36f3254, #6783).
  • Core: Long cut-list edits play every cut without dropped or late frames (9fd90d23c, #6785).
  • CLI: Hyperframes docs motion-blur shows the motion blur guide (21eefcd00, #6779).

Docs & Examples

  • Studio App: Pending video edits have no cap of 10 (f43bec76e, #6797).

  • CLI: A missing registry item names the closest ones, and a blocked browser says how to allow it (47a1a9206, #6799).
  • Registry: Caption components keep the last phrase until its last word ends (32c01c99a, #6792).

Internal

  • Studio: Edit accuracy screenshots are viewport-only and taken in the confirmation re-runs (36cf990d9, #6795).
  • CLI: A failed late-image snapshot reports why it failed (27e16becb, #6793).
  • Studio: Split edit accuracy into 15 PR shards packed by measured case time (2a9dd0598, #6784).

hugohe3/ppt-master ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v6.8.0 Stable2026-10-30T06:05:44Z

The OpenAI image backend now defaults to gpt-image-2.5-sunburst, and image generation accepts any W:H aspect ratio. This release also brings a round of Edit Native, template-import, and source-conversion fixes that stop content from being silently dropped, plus PowerPoint-exact animation timing.

AI image generation

  • The OpenAI backend now defaults to gpt-image-2.5-sunburst. Measured on the official endpoint at 1024×1024 medium, it used about a quarter of the output tokens of gpt-image-2 (~$0.013 vs ~$0.053) and returned in about half the time. It also returns real RGBA for OPENAI_BACKGROUND=transparent. OPENAI_MODEL still overrides it (fc7aa7c5, 27be992f, af5029c7).
  • aspect_ratio accepts any positive W:H, decimals included (e.g. 1.85:1). Unsupported ratios no longer fail a request: each backend picks the nearest size its model allows, reports the adaptation, and placement crops to the requested shape (b1b7f1b2, be8a19da, e61c34ed).
  • Rate limits are handled properly. Retry-After is honored by every backend and by manifest requeues. Waits are capped so that a huge header value can no longer crash the run or block it for a day, and a run that stops with items still pending exits non-zero (c02689f4, 230afab1, 4c3961a8, 625e05a9).
  • --filename values containing dots (gpt-image-2.5-sunburst_hero) are no longer truncated at the last dot, which made each such run silently overwrite the previous file (c02689f4).

Edit Native PPTX

  • When only a chart's data changes, the imported chart is now patched in place instead of being rebuilt from JSON. Gradient fills, slice separators, legend layout, per-point data labels, marker outlines, and style and theme overrides now survive the edit (78759bf6).
  • Charts that do get rebuilt keep the gap width, overlap, inner layout, axis label typography, and chart language. A rebuild no longer borrows an unrelated frame (0396656f). Theme font tokens such as +mn-ea stay in their East Asian and complex-script slots (78fcef9b). Charts whose source does not vary point colors are no longer imported as varied (351041e4).
  • Editing any cell of an imported table with merged cells no longer makes --native-charts-and-tables fail (48ee45f9).
  • New objects on a rebuilt slide keep their SVG stacking order. A new full-canvas rect is kept as an object instead of being dropped as a background (bc111480, 523e5bd9).

PPTX import and Create Template

  • Real-world PPTX files with zero-size geometry no longer hard-fail. This covers horizontal and vertical rules, collinear groups, and vertical CJK text groups. Geometry that cannot be recovered is kept and reported instead of silently dropped. Thanks @SunshineLin-li (#329; 5832c3a8, 6965c0db, cafb4ccf, 83ac084b, ce1a6222, e8ac905f).
  • Theme line styles (a:lnRef) now resolve to the theme's width, cap, dash, and alpha instead of a 1px stroke (83ac084b). Imported custom-geometry connectors export correctly (3b865783).
  • Content the author hid in PowerPoint no longer leaks into source Markdown, the Beautify inventory, or rebuilt speaker notes, and each exclusion is reported (2cdae003).
  • Beautify now includes chart titles in its frozen inventory and verifier, so a dropped title fails --verify (694bdaca).

Source conversion (source_to_md)

  • PowerPoint: Office Math is kept as LaTeX, and the Fallback of mc:AlternateContent (3D models, ink, zoom) is read. Chart titles and picture alt text are used as labels (05011292, fd17372d, 6d1cb8ff).
  • DOCX: text now matches what Word displays. Simple-field results (caption numbers, document properties) are kept, hidden runs are excluded, and footnote and endnote math is converted (1ad85c14).
  • EPUB, ODT, and RTF: text the readers skipped is recovered, including EPUB footnote files (f3660bfc). HTML <template> markup no longer leaks into page text (b7a2d9fc).

Animations

  • Animation timing now follows PowerPoint's beat semantics. An after-previous row waits for every effect in its click group to finish, every with-previous delay counts from the beat start, and each beat's rows are grouped under one shared timing node, matching what PowerPoint writes. Thanks @Victor-Quqi (#331; 5b7e56b9, e897e762, dea06b93, 6615a22b).

Finalize and live preview editor

  • Splitting text rows, and other finalize or editor rewrites, now keep the element's id, name, role, visibility, and references, so hidden text no longer becomes visible and locators no longer break (06f0d112).

Quality checker

  • Hyperlinks are compared after XML decoding, so &amp; in a query string, an alternate xlink prefix, or a numeric character reference no longer raises a false outline-hyperlink error. Thanks @I1eanch (#338).

Installation and docs

  • Supported Python is now capped at 3.14. The Windows guide adds troubleshooting for source builds: on 3.15, PyYAML has no wheel yet and pip falls back to compiling it (15baa7c5).
  • The spec-lock reference states that icons.inventory is none when no icon is prepared (687a00af).

View release →

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.245 Stable2026-10-30T05:34:13Z

A faster Orca, plugins & background computer use ⚡

  • ⚡ A faster Orca: Launch smoothly with hundreds of chats, with less lag as you type and switch tabs.
  • 🧩 Customize Orca with icon themes, syntax highlighting and custom editors.
  • 🖱️ Keep working while agents use Chrome, Blender and other Mac apps in the background.
  • 🎙️ Dictate on your phone and see your words as you speak.
  • 💬 Try the new Chat UI: Search for “Chat UI” in Settings to turn it on, then right-click a tab to switch live between Chat UI and TUI.

Highlights

  • A faster Orca: Fast app with hundreds of chats. Typing stays more responsive, and hidden chats do less work while you use other tabs.
  • Plugins: Add icon themes, syntax highlighting and custom editors from Settings → Plugins. Community publishing is coming soon, so you’ll be able to share your own plugins.
  • Background computer use on Mac: Let agents work in Chrome, Blender and other apps while you keep using your mouse and keyboard. Orca’s in-app browser already works in the background; now computer use does too. Some actions still need the app brought forward.
  • Chats: Enjoy a very polished Chat UI, send again after Stop, and preview links in chat. Search for “Chat UI” in Settings to turn it on, and right-click a tab to switch live between Chat UI and TUI.
  • Terminals: Set a running terminal limit for each computer in Settings → Terminal → Advanced.
  • WSL: Stopped distros stay stopped until you choose Start. Terminals remember their folder.
  • Browser: Press Cmd+Opt+B on Mac for a browser split. Send shorter annotation feedback, now with pasted images.
  • Workspaces: Your workspace name becomes its branch name again. Source Control actions get Model and Effort controls.
  • Phone: See dictation as you speak, and read clearer code, tables and diagrams.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • New terminal host: This release starts a fresh terminal host for new terminals. Terminals already open before the update keep running on the previous host and stay attached. The new running terminal limit setting needs the new host, and so do fixes such as the ble.sh startup fix and lighter process checks on macOS. Those apply to terminals you open after updating.
  • Plugins are on by default: Installing a plugin, including from an orca://plugins link, only opens a review screen first. Plugins that run code ask for your consent before they install, and once approved they run with full access to your computer.
  • Running terminal limit: The default now follows each computer: half of its system terminal limit, up to 4096, or 256 as before when Orca can’t read that limit. A value you set in Settings or with an environment variable takes priority. Raising or lowering it never closes running terminals. A remote server running an older Orca keeps working, but the limit can’t be changed there until it updates.
  • Chat naming: Chats are now named by their own agent and account by default. A naming agent, custom command or arguments you saved before keep working, and the naming agent choice moved under Advanced in Settings → Chat → Chat naming.
  • Codex history across accounts: A new switch, Settings → Agents → Share Codex history across accounts, is on by default and keeps today’s behavior. With it on, history from Orca’s managed accounts now also shows up in your own ~/.codex when you switch back to system default. Turning it off stops new sharing and removes nothing. On an SSH or WSL host running an older Orca, the switch stays disabled until that host updates.
  • Codex on macOS and Linux: Orca now starts Codex with its paste guess turned off, so prompts typed from a phone or another client send instead of staying in the composer. Real pastes still arrive as pastes.
  • Computer use on a Mac: Agents now act in the background by default. Agents can still ask for the old foreground mode with --restore-window, which drags into Chromium and Electron apps still need.
  • Browser annotations: Send and Copy now give agents a short summary per note. Check Include page details in the Send menu to get the old full output; the choice lasts for that browser pane.
  • Workspace branch names: A name you type for a new workspace is used as its branch again, even when Auto-rename branch from work is on. Auto-rename only replaces names Orca made up.
  • Automations: Agents started by an automation skip their CLI’s startup update prompt, so the prompt can’t swallow the task. Agents you start yourself are unchanged.
  • Source Control actions: Model and effort flags saved in an action’s Arguments move into the new Model and Effort controls when Orca loads; other Arguments stay as they are. A phone or remote server on an older Orca may launch these actions with a different model or effort until it updates.
  • New shortcuts: Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+X opens Plugins (outside text fields), and Cmd+Opt+B on macOS opens a browser tab in a split to the right. Cmd+Opt+B can be changed in Settings → Keyboard.
  • Known limitation, downgrading: If you go back to v1.4.244, custom model and effort choices on Source Control AI actions reset to the agent’s defaults, and updating again won’t bring them back.

Product experience

Native chat

No more startup freeze with many chats, link previews, naming with the chat’s own agent, and a working Send after Stop.

  • Show chats as Interrupted or Done at startup without reading their history, so Orca no longer freezes after launch with hundreds of chats by @brennanb2025 in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/32146
  • Show hover cards for GitHub PR and issue links in chats by @AmoabaKelvin in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/35199

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-10-30T05:20:59Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-1964+ge0516a46e8
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1792527110

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

View release →

openclaw/openclaw ↗ 3 releases · 2 stable · 1 prerelease
v2026.10.10-beta.5openclaw 2026.10.10-beta.5 Prerelease2026-10-30T03:57:14Z

2026.10.10-beta.5

Highlights

  • Node compatibility: installs and updates work again on Node >=24.16.0 <25 || >=26.1.0; Node 24.22.1 or 26.12.1 is now a security recommendation instead of a hard requirement, and Node 26 workers no longer abort when stdin is a file. (#186589, #187516) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Gateway streaming: replies that contain tool-call-like XML, look like tool calls, or end long HTML at a partial tag no longer stall the Gateway. (#181397, #183252, #180765; related #175156, #180720) Thanks @RomneyDa, @JasonBuildAI, and @LeizhenZhang.
  • Updates and rollback: a failed npm rollback no longer strands the installed package, an immediate Git rollback keeps operator edits, and an update whose executor dies recovers its offline state. (#185160, #185201, #181695; related #184889, #184887, #181649) Thanks @LiuwqGit, @RomneyDa, @syxscott, @wr-web, and @PonySlayer28.
  • Cron: missed slots with a stale stored next run time are no longer dropped, and startup catch-up runs are admitted after a restart. (#179928; related #179927) Thanks @andrewbowlds, @RomneyDa, and @shakkernerd.

Changes

Fixes

v2026.9.36openclaw 2026.9.36 Stable2026-10-29T20:19:38Z

This is a gateway-only extended-stable release, which is our current equivalent to LTS. This release is OpenClaw from the end of September 2026, plus critical security updates, reliability and performance fixes, and features like new model support. The latest version of OpenClaw at the time of this release is 2026.10.9

2026.9.36

Highlights

  • Failed updates stay recoverable: a failed npm rollback no longer leaves the installed package missing, and update checks keep Doctor recovery guidance, stop recopying private rehearsal databases, and no longer reopen candidate-migrated state. Fixes #184889. (#185160, #186177) Thanks @syxscott, @LiuwqGit, and @RomneyDa.
  • Workspace restrictions reach Gateway-built tools: with tools.fs.workspaceOnly, openclaw delegates built through Gateway loopback and skill dispatch now require approval as intended, and media tools stay inside the workspace. (#185094) Thanks @eleqtrizit and @RomneyDa.
  • Microsoft Teams allowlists stay restrictive: name-keyed teams or groupAllowFrom entries that do not resolve now grant nothing instead of opening the bot to every team or group. (#184989) Thanks @eleqtrizit, @RomneyDa, and @shakkernerd.
  • Untrusted input is bounded: malformed media metadata, inbound mail addresses, TOML, URIs, oversized collections in custom A2UI schemas and Discord's embedded SDK, and response-controlled Microsoft authentication routing can no longer cause unbounded work or alter identity. (#187288, #187282, #187283, #187305, #187289, #187287) Thanks @RomneyDa and @cyyynthia.
  • Replies keep their text: comparison prose after emoji and other astral characters is no longer stripped from outbound replies. (#187415) Thanks @RomneyDa.

Changes

  • Certificate trust and Docker images: the eMudhra trust remediation is applied, and Docker release images carry source/version-bound evidence with more reliable architecture builds. (#186639) Thanks @RomneyDa.

Fixes

  • Credential and content boundaries: derive the filesystem policy for Gateway-built OpenClaw tools, keep unresolved Microsoft Teams allowlists closed, and scope the patched MSAL to the affected Teams and Azure Identity consumers. (#185094, #184989, #187287) Thanks @eleqtrizit, @RomneyDa, and @shakkernerd.
  • Untrusted parsing: bound Matrix media metadata and IMAP mail parsing, the TOML key parser, URI parsing, and oversized Zod collections in custom A2UI schemas and Discord's embedded SDK. (#187288, #187282, #187283, #187305, #187289) Thanks @RomneyDa and @cyyynthia.
  • Reply delivery: keep comparison prose after astral characters in the outbound sanitizer. (#187415) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • xAI: allow off for older models without configurable reasoning effort instead of rejecting it as an invalid thinking level. (#187425) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Update, install, and Doctor: restore the installed package root after a denied rollback rename, keep Doctor recovery guidance and migration rollback restrictions, preserve cleanup uncertainty in buffered command results, keep the update compatibility inventory out of published packages, and stop retrying broken worktree metadata on every cleanup sweep. Fixes #184889, #184237. (#185160, #186177, #184270, #187424, #187475) Thanks @syxscott, @shakkernerd, @LiuwqGit, and @RomneyDa.
  • Node uploads: recheck cancellation after opening a staged upload snapshot so a cancelled upload no longer surfaces an AbortError. (#162567) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Dependencies: update the ClawHub undici pin, Vercel toolchain packages, and the Vitest cohort on this line. (#187261, #187258, #187275) Thanks @RomneyDa.

Complete contribution record

This audited record covers the complete v2026.9.35..d04a9bec6c286f60f106d6f61354bad4e8b9a057 history: 28 in-range PRs + 0 retained seed-only PRs = 28 unique PRs. The generation manifest also supplies direct commits as editorial input; the grouped notes above prioritize user impact.

v2026.10.9openclaw 2026.10.9 Stable2026-10-29T15:54:35Z

2026.10.9

Highlights

  • Patch vulnerable runtime dependencies: fast-uri 4.2.3 rejects malformed URI schemes and hosts in MCP schema validation, oversized Zod collections are rejected early in A2UI and the Discord embedded app SDK, IMAP sender parsing uses nodemailer 10.1.4 so spoofed senders cannot pass the sender token gate, and Microsoft Teams uses patched MSAL Node 7.1.1. (#187294) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Apply tools.fs.workspaceOnly to Gateway-built tools, so delegate changes still require approval and media tools stay inside the workspace. (#185094) Thanks @eleqtrizit, @RomneyDa, and @shakkernerd.
  • Keep live credentials out of MCP CLI inspection and mask Google OAuth client secrets in logs. (#180321, #180308, #183193) Thanks @brandon-julio-t, @wr-web, @LeizhenZhang, and @RomneyDa.
  • Make failed update rollbacks safe: Git-source rollbacks preserve operator edits instead of resetting the checkout, and npm rollbacks retry transient Windows denials and report the exact move that restores a bootable install instead of stranding the package root. (#185201, #184887, #185160, #184889) Thanks @wr-web, @syxscott, @LiuwqGit, @shakkernerd, and @RomneyDa.
  • Recover intact agent databases and stale full-text indexes after SQLite tokenizer changes, including update snapshots. (#176234, #176220, #176833, #181206) Thanks @ndakota79 and @RomneyDa.
  • Repair publication-complete updates after a remount while retaining the original launcher and sealed recovery helper. (#186557, #186527) Thanks @ericcaiwx-star, @sennyhoney, and @RomneyDa.
  • Migrate large transcript archives without exceeding Node.js string limits. (#170853, #170847) Thanks @iamonlv426 and @RomneyDa.
  • Allow agents to recover after a full disk once space becomes available, without requiring a restart. (#176902) Thanks @RomneyDa.

Changes

This maintenance release focuses on security dependency patches, recovery, data preservation, and reliability fixes.

Fixes

  • Resolve local media in Codex steering messages from the session's own workspace instead of the agent's default workspace. (#181528) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Report uncertain process cleanup from buffered commands, such as local CLI speech providers, instead of presenting it as an ordinary command failure. (#184270, #184237) Thanks @shakkernerd and @RomneyDa.
  • Keep reapproved node connections alive by publishing the approval with its committed pairing facts before the request resumes. Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Recover stopped Gateways when service activation cannot be verified; retain untouched-recovery behavior in Windows triage. (#172784, #181577, #181698) Thanks @SMF12138 and @RomneyDa.
  • Avoid uninstalling a Gateway service during a passive macOS stop when no service is installed. (#181257) Thanks @shakkernerd, @LiuwqGit, @eleqtrizit, @syxscott, and @RomneyDa.
  • Recover CLI Node runtimes when launched from the filesystem root, including launchd jobs. (#178112, #177739) Thanks @downingkeys and @RomneyDa.
  • Start new ACP sessions in the requested working directory instead of the default workspace. (#176852, #123557) Thanks @yinx65588-boop, @crappier, and @RomneyDa.
  • Prevent overlapping model refreshes from failing requests. (#179144) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Honor an explicit SSH port 22 in openclaw gateway probe --ssh instead of the alias's configured port. (#171092) Thanks @zhangguiping-xydt, @obviyus, and @RomneyDa.
  • Keep skill metadata such as icons and homepages when an inline YAML metadata value has a trailing comment. (#171224) Thanks @zhangguiping-xydt, @Takhoffman, and @RomneyDa.
  • Show provider-ignored options in openclaw infer video generate text and JSON output. (#171172) Thanks @ly85206559, @obviyus, and @RomneyDa.

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.22CC Switch v4.0.22 Stable2026-10-30T03:51:34Z

CC Switch v4.0.22

本版本新增对 DeepSeek Harness 的支持，它是 CC Switch 管理的第 14 个应用：供应商一起写进它的 Web 和 Desktop 配置，MCP、Skills、提示词同步给它，会话可以浏览，「应用」页可以安装、升级、卸载；还可以在授权中心登录 DeepSeek 账号、查看余额，用量也会统计。Oh My Pi 的用量现在也会统计。「打开终端」可以勾选跳过权限确认、追加启动参数，Codex 卡片也能打开终端；WebDAV 配置可以清除；Skills 可以打开目录、在发现页查看文档；GitHub Copilot 的 Claude 模型改走原生接口，提示缓存命中率大幅提高。另外修复了 macOS 上连了 OAuth 远程 MCP 时切换 Claude 账号失败、开了全局代理时添加 Claude 账号报 403、Claude Desktop 新会话默认用 Fable、MiniMax 周额度用完后不显示、没在用的供应商余额不自动刷新、请求被转回路由自己导致内存暴涨、Gemini 工具调用报 400、xAI 拒绝 Codex 0.163 的请求、请求日志里存下整页报错把数据库撑大等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

DeepSeek Harness

DeepSeek Harness（命令是 dsh）现在是一个独立的应用，有自己的供应商、MCP、Skills、提示词和会话（#8795、#8986）。配置在 ~/.dsh，设了 DSH_HOME 时以它为准。

  • Web 和 Desktop 一起管理。 DeepSeek Harness 的网页版和桌面版各有一份配置（profile），CC Switch 把供应商和 MCP 同时写进两份，缺的那份会自动建好；已经初始化的 headless、acp 也一起写。侧栏只有一个 DeepSeek Harness，「已添加」标题旁注明 Web 和 Desktop 共用这份列表。某一份被手动改得和另一份不一样时，编辑会被拒绝并提示，不会覆盖你的改动。
  • 供应商可以同时添加。 和 OpenCode 一样是「添加」「移除」，多个供应商同时写进去，在 DeepSeek Harness 里选模型。支持 OpenAI Chat Completions、OpenAI Responses、Anthropic Messages 三种接口，预设来自 Pi 的目录。API Key 写进 DeepSeek Harness 自己的凭据文件 .credentials.yaml，配置里只放引用；配置里的其他插件设置原样保留，写入失败会回滚。
  • 模型编辑。 思考、图片输入用开关，上下文长度和最大输出单独填，和 Pi、Kimi Code 的模型行一样；DeepSeek Harness 还可以设置可选的思考档位和每个模型的接口兼容选项。OpenCode、MiniMax Code 的模型编辑也改成了这个样子。
  • DeepSeek 账号登录。 在授权中心的 DeepSeek Harness 组用浏览器登录 DeepSeek 账号，显示余额和赠金；Web 和 Desktop 共用这份登录，在任一处登录或退出，两边同时生效。添加供应商时选「DeepSeek 官方登录」会跳到授权中心。登录后，账号以一张官方卡显示在「已添加」最前面，账号下的模型由 DeepSeek Harness 自己提供，这张卡不能编辑，退出登录后自动消失。
  • MCP、Skills、提示词。 支持 stdio 和 Streamable HTTP 两种 MCP。
  • 会话和用量。 可以浏览 DeepSeek Harness 的会话（含思考过程和工具调用）；恢复和删除会话请在 DeepSeek Harness 里操作。用量从会话记录导入，走 DeepSeek 账号的请求在用量页单独显示为「DeepSeek 账号（OAuth）」。
  • 「应用」页管理 DeepSeek Harness。 用 npm 安装、升级、卸载 @deepseek-ai/dsh，卸载不删你的数据。
  • DeepSeek Harness 不经过本地路由。

Oh My Pi 的用量统计

用量页现在会统计 Oh My Pi 的用量，有自己的应用分类（#9046、#8939）。子代理的用量、对话之外的模型调用也会算进去，首次导入会包括以前的子代理会话。Oh My Pi 和 Pi 用同一个会话目录时，各自只导入自己的会话。Pi 已导入的数据不用重新导入。

「打开终端」可以跳过权限确认、追加启动参数

「打开终端」对话框多了两项（#9008）：

  • 跳过权限确认。 勾选后 Claude Code 和 Antigravity 以 --dangerously-skip-permissions 启动，Codex 以 --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox 启动。默认不勾。
  • 附加参数（可选）。 填 --debug、--append-system-prompt "Be concise" 这类参数，接在启动命令后面。用空格分隔，含空格的值用引号括起来，最多 16 个。参数只作为参数传给程序，不会被当成命令执行。

两项都只用于这次打开的终端，不写进任何配置；终端打开成功后，CC Switch 会按应用记住你的选择。

Codex 卡片也能「打开终端」

以前 Codex 卡片上没有这个按钮。现在第三方卡片打开的终端只用这张卡：以 codex -c ... 启动，不改 config.toml，Key 通过环境变量传入，不出现在命令行里。当前供应商在 config.toml 里留下的设置会尽量改成不影响这张卡的值，改不了的（比如请求头、指定的 profile）会提示先切到这张卡再打开，避免当前供应商的 Key 被发到别处。当前使用中的卡直接启动 codex。

清除 WebDAV 配置

以前配好 WebDAV 同步后，只能关掉自动同步，地址、账号、密码一直留在本机（#8977）。现在 WebDAV 同步设置里有「清除配置」按钮，确认后从本机删除这些信息并停止自动同步；云端已上传的数据不会删除。

Skills：打开目录、复制名称、查看文档

  • 已安装的 Skill（#8993）：⋯ 菜单里可以「打开目录」，在文件管理器里打开这个 Skill 的文件夹；点击 Skill 名称就能复制名称。原来的「复制目录名」去掉了。
  • 发现页（#9039）：每一行多了「打开文档」按钮，安装前可以先看说明，没有文档时打开仓库主页。

改进

GitHub Copilot 的 Claude 模型提示缓存命中率大幅提高（#8908）。以前 Copilot 的 Claude 模型一律转成 Chat Completions 格式发送，会丢掉缓存标记，Claude Code 在对话中途插入的系统消息还会打断缓存。现在 Copilot 支持原生 Anthropic 接口的模型直接走原生接口，Copilot 企业版长会话的缓存命中率实测从约 35% 提高到约 95%。

添加、启用、停用、删除 Skill 仓库时，只重新读取这一个仓库（#8907、#1426），不再把所有仓库重新下载一遍。点刷新按钮仍会全部重新读取。

托盘和用量设置里也能看到绑了账号的 Claude 官方卡额度。 以前只有卡片上显示，托盘里没有，用量设置弹窗还显示成未启用。

Claude Desktop 的路由服务也接受 x-api-key 传令牌（#5123），令牌校验改成固定耗时的比较（#7488、#7482）。

Claude 配置目录在 WSL 里时，授权中心直接说明暂不支持 Claude 账号，「添加账号」「重新登录」不可用。以前会让你改用「打开终端」，但这时打开的是 Windows 里的 Claude Code，同样用不了。

MCP、Skills 列表悬停勾选框时不再同时弹出两个提示；问号帮助卡片改为显示在右边，不再挡住鼠标，长内容会换行、在卡片里滚动（#8593）。

从别处跳到授权中心的某一组时，页面其他部分不再跟着移动（#8964）。

OpenCode、OpenClaw、Hermes 的配置文件读不了时，供应商页会显示错误（#9044）。以前页面像从没配置过一样空着，添加供应商失败后才看到报错，还会留下一条多余的记录。

托管的 ChatGPT、xAI 账号登录过期时，提示改为「请在授权中心重新登录这个账号」。 以前提示运行一条并不存在的命令。

预设： 新增 OspreyAI、讯飞星火 Astron Coding Plan 的 Claude Code 预设（#9036）；TokenFlux 改用新域名 tokenflux.com，tokenflux.dev 作为备用地址。

修复

  • macOS 上连了需要 OAuth 登录的远程 MCP 时，切换 Claude 账号报「Claude Code 的登录信息太大，无法写入钥匙串，没有切换」（#9002）。MCP 的登录信息和账号存在同一个钥匙串条目里，超过了一次写入的长度上限。现在超长时改用和 Claude Code 一样的写法。
  • 开了全局代理时，在授权中心添加 Claude 账号，浏览器登录成功后报 403（#9038）。换取令牌是 Claude Code 自己完成的，以前它没有用到全局代理。现在 CC Switch 运行 Claude Code 登录、查额度时会把全局代理传给它（SOCKS 代理 Claude Code 不支持，不传）。
  • Claude Desktop 新会话默认用 Fable，而且模型顺序每次启用配置都会变（#7491、#7457、#8973）。Claude Desktop 把模型列表里的第一个当默认模型，以前按字母顺序写。现在按界面上 Sonnet、Opus、Fable、Haiku 的顺序写，新会话默认用 Sonnet 档。
  • MiniMax 周额度用完后，卡片和托盘里不再显示周额度，5 小时额度刚重置时看起来像额度全满（#8124、#8123）。现在显示为红色的「已用完」和周额度重置倒计时。
  • 没在用的供应商设了自动查询间隔，余额也不刷新（#8342、#8335）。
  • 上游地址填成了路由自己的地址时，请求被无限转回路由，内存不断增长（#8598）。现在直接报配置错误。
  • Claude Code 经路由连 Gemini 时，工具调用后报 400：签名放错位置报「Unknown name thoughtSignature」（#6690）；CC Switch 重启后或较早的轮次丢了签名，Gemini 3 以上拒绝请求（#6833）；Vertex 上工具结果后面跟着文字时报「Requests ending with a model turn are not supported」（#8988）。
  • Claude Code 经路由转 OpenAI Responses 时，WebSearch 带屏蔽域名就直接报 400（#8979）。现在 API Key 接入的 Responses 上游会原样传过去；ChatGPT 官方登录的路由不支持屏蔽域名，仍会明确报错。
  • Codex 0.163 以上经路由连 xAI 时报 400（#8997、#8996）。
  • Codex 的 config.toml 里有 [[skills.config]] 这类数组表时，第三方供应商的地址被写进了这张表里（#9029、#9023）。
  • OpenCode 新格式（V2）的供应商「检测连通」报缺少 options.baseURL（#8969）。
  • 上游报错时，请求日志存下整页 HTML 报错，数据库和备份越来越大（#9010、#6706）。现在每条只存前 4096 个字符；已经存下的旧记录这次不清理。
  • Windows 上用 MyDockFinder 这类第三方任务栏时，点托盘图标没反应（#8729、#8565）。
  • Kimi Code 0.43.0 以前的版本在「应用」页升级后版本不变（#9032）。旧版本没有自动确认升级的选项，现在改用安装它的包管理器，或重新运行官方安装脚本。
  • 「应用」页里，程序文件在但运行不起来的应用，分在「已安装」组里却显示「未安装」。 现在显示为「无法运行」，并给出原因。
  • Antigravity 的 CLI 和 IDE 都装了时，在 IDE 里继续的旧会话用量不更新、会话打不开（#8941）。

升级说明

数据库版本从 23 升到 24，为 MCP 和 Skills 增加 DeepSeek Harness 的开关。升级前会自动备份数据库；升级后再退回旧版本，旧版本会提示数据库版本过新，打不开。

  • DeepSeek Harness 默认显示在侧栏，不用的话可以在「应用」页关掉「在侧栏显示 DeepSeek Harness」。

致谢

感谢 @funkpopo、@yang227、@Byzhazha、@YUZHEthefool（DeepSeek Harness 支持、账号登录与用量统计）、@shimenga、@allenxu09（Oh My Pi 用量统计；allenxu09 还做了第三方任务栏的托盘点击、Skill 仓库按需读取）、@linghaoSu（Copilot 原生接口）、@hekuanga（Gemini 签名位置）、@SailingLoong（Claude Desktop 模型顺序）、@John1Tang、@yukitakasama（Claude Desktop 令牌校验）、@cenglin123（MiniMax 周额度）、@deefery（xAI 请求）、@szupzj18（Codex 数组表）、@zayokami（请求日志报错截断）的贡献。funkpopo 还做了 Skills 打开目录、余额自动刷新、帮助提示、路由自环检测和授权中心跳转。

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.56.0.

View release →

studio-v2.56.0Reasonix Studio v2.56.0 Stable2026-10-30T00:29:32Z

本版新增项目栏「最近」跨项目找会话、每日定时深色模式和可关闭的输入框提示，并修复从旧版找回会话全部被跳过、更新失败后无法下载完整安装包等多处问题。

新增

  • 项目栏新增「最近」：按最近活动时间跨项目列出会话，完整分页、可搜索；点选后定位到所属项目并打开会话，也可选择远程环境。关联 #14319 fixed in #14784 by @lancy69。
  • 「外观 → 明暗」新增「定时」模式：每天按本机时间切换深色，默认 20:00 到次日 07:00，可跨午夜；桌面窗口的边框和底色同步变化。只有选择它时才生效。关联 #14699 fixed in #14783 by @lancy69。
  • 「外观 → 会话显示」新增「输入框提示」开关：可隐藏输入框里的任务和排队占位文字，默认显示，只保存在本机。关联 #14759 fixed in #14781 by @lancy69。
  • 文档：使用指南新增跨项目任务派发的完整流程。关联 #14603 fixed in #14780 by @lancy69。

变更

  • 浏览器工具的说明现在提示模型读静态网页时用 web_fetch、用完关闭标签，避免一轮留下十几个页面。修复 #14737 fixed in #14764。
  • 设置里「快捷键」页的卡片顺序调整：「Esc 停止任务」紧跟发送相关设置，全局快捷键和按键表放在后面。关联 #14789 fixed in #14790。

修复

  • 从旧版找回会话时，目标文件夹里残留没有对话文件的过期索引，会让所有会话被反复跳过、一个都导入不了；现在会清掉它们并正常导入，侧栏里的未导入列表也能显示。修复 #14804 fixed in #14806。
  • 更新失败后提示里的「下载完整安装包」改为用系统浏览器打开发布页，不再落到被设置页盖住的内置浏览器里。修复 #14775 fixed in #14788。
  • 侧栏里「早先版本」和「恢复副本」那一行，悬停或键盘聚焦时重新显示删除按钮 ×。修复 #14703 fixed in #14803 by @xxoingr。
  • 删除配置来源文件夹后，仍可移除残留的 MCP 服务；配置损坏时会保留服务，并指出无法解析的配置文件。关联 #14738 fixed in #14749 by @lancy69、#14739 fixed in #14749 by @lancy69。
  • 连接远程主机（如 WSL）时，读取会话列表、删除、置顶或归档不再每次都重新建立连接；连接在最后一次操作后保留 45 秒。修复 #14745 fixed in #14763。
  • 从对话分叉出的分支会继承源会话的上下文压缩简报，打开后继续在压缩视图上工作。关联 #14768 by @jf200s。
  • 同一项目里并行启动的隔离任务不再偶尔因 Git 报 failed to read …/commondir 而失败。关联 #14805 by @xxoingr。
  • 隔离结果应用、委派续跑和用量预算：应用时拒绝被换成目录链接的父目录，并发续跑不再重复创建转录副本，已知用量计入根预算，恢复任务时文件权限变化也会使旧证明失效。关联 #14025。

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@lancy69、@xxoingr、@jf200s

<!-- lang:en -->

New

  • The project header gains "Recent": conversations across projects by last activity, paged and searchable; picking one locates its project and opens it, also on remote hosts. Refs #14319 fixed in #14784 by @lancy69.
  • Appearance → Light and dark gains a "Scheduled" mode: dark during a daily local-clock range (default 20:00–07:00, overnight allowed); the window frame follows. Opt-in. Refs #14699 fixed in #14783 by @lancy69.
  • Appearance → Conversation display gains a "Composer hint" switch that hides the task and queue placeholder text; on by default, remembered on this machine. Refs #14759 fixed in #14781 by @lancy69.
  • Docs: the guide now walks through cross-project task dispatch. Refs #14603 fixed in #14780 by @lancy69.

Changed

  • The browser tool now tells the model to read static pages with web_fetch and to close tabs when done, so a turn no longer leaves a dozen pages open. Fixes #14737 fixed in #14764.
  • On the Shortcuts settings page, "Esc to stop" now sits right after the send settings, with the global shortcut and the key table after it. Refs #14789 fixed in #14790.

Fixed

  • Recovering older sessions no longer skips every conversation because of stale indexes with no transcript in the destination; they are cleared and the import proceeds. Fixes #14804 fixed in #14806.
  • After a failed update, "download the full installer" opens the release page in the system browser instead of the in-app browser behind the settings sheet. Fixes #14775 fixed in #14788.
  • An earlier version or recovery copy in the sidebar shows its delete × again on hover or keyboard focus. Fixes #14703 fixed in #14803 by @xxoingr.
  • Stale MCP servers can be removed after their source folder is deleted; a malformed config names the file and keeps the servers. Refs #14738 fixed in #14749 by @lancy69, #14739 fixed in #14749 by @lancy69.
  • On a remote host such as WSL, listing, deleting, pinning or archiving sessions no longer reconnects every time; the link is held 45 seconds after the last action. Fixes #14745 fixed in #14763.
  • A fork inherits its source's compacted context, so the branch continues on the compacted view. Refs #14768 by @jf200s.
  • Isolated tasks started in parallel in one project no longer fail now and then with Git's "failed to read …/commondir". Refs #14805 by @xxoingr.
  • Isolated results and delegation: applying refuses a parent directory swapped for a link, parallel continuations no longer duplicate copies, and known usage counts toward budgets. Refs #14025.

Contributors

Thanks to this release's contributors: @lancy69, @xxoingr, @jf200s

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.14.0 Stable2026-10-29T18:25:10Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Breaking Changes

  • Removed the Bun event-loop workaround utilities from @oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core/utils/yield; agent loops no longer insert cooperative sleeps while streaming or between tool calls.

Added

  • Added AgentTool.approvalRestrictionPaths so tools can report the paths they read, allowing restricted-folder approvals to cover those paths without remembering approval separately for each path.
  • Added Agent.logContext, which adds custom context fields to every log record written during an agent run.

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Breaking Changes

  • Moved Anthropic header and endpoint helpers, along with Codex session/transport inspection and OpenAI Responses replay/storage helpers, to dedicated provider modules. Update imports from the previous locations.
  • Updated auth-gateway model resolvers to receive ModelResolveOptions, including the route's native API wire and the scoped client's accepted grants. Scoped client IDs no longer fall back to scanning all models; resolvers now select the best permitted candidate.

Added

  • Added mTLS support for Anthropic connections through the CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_CERT and CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_KEY environment variables.

Changed

  • Provider implementations are now loaded on demand, reducing startup and memory overhead while preserving existing stream timeouts, cancellation, and host overrides.
  • Account policy priority now takes precedence when usage information is temporarily unavailable, keeping new sessions on the preferred account.
  • Improved provider-specific credential usage lookups so they no longer wait for unrelated providers, while preserving cache freshness, cancellation isolation, and complete aggregate reports. The corresponding server-side latency improvement requires an updated auth broker.
  • Credential usage is now prefetched during session setup without selecting, pinning, or advancing the OAuth account rotation.

Fixed

  • Fixed Mistral thinking-message replay requests that could be rejected by LiteLLM with a 422 extra_forbidden error.
  • Fixed native Judge API requests missing the omp/<version> User-Agent, so omp usage is correctly identified in upstream statistics.
  • Fixed Codex capacity-error retries over WebSocket and SSE ignoring provider-supplied Retry-After delays and retrying too early.
  • Fixed cancelled OAuth refresh requests aborting the entire session, and ensured requests joining an existing refresh can still use the refreshed credential when the original request is cancelled.
  • Fixed Cursor models losing omp system-prompt context, including context files, skills, and rules, on subsequent turns or when earlier messages precede the active turn.
  • Fixed short-lived commands that refresh OAuth credentials, such as omp token --force-refresh, remaining open for several seconds after completing.
  • Fixed TypeSafe authentication when TYPESAFE_BASE_URL is configured by validating keys against the correct /v1/models endpoint.
  • Fixed forced tool calls on non-reasoning models causing an unhandled reasoning-effort error.
  • Fixed interrupted turns reporting a transport error instead of the caller-provided cancellation reason.
  • Fixed long-running pi-native turns being terminated before their first event by fetch or proxy timeouts by supporting keepalives and the configured first-event timeout.
  • Fixed pi-native clients crashing when the auth gateway reports an upstream failure during streaming.
  • Fixed Cursor turns failing after a mid-turn stream interruption when an automatic retry receives a zero-token provider error; retries now continue on the rotated conversation.
  • Corrected auth-gateway debug logs so they no longer claim a retry occurred when a usage-limit error was returned without retrying.

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Breaking Changes

  • Removed the raw @oh-my-pi/pi-catalog/models.json export; use the bundled model registry APIs instead.

Added

  • Added nextBuildPriceChange(cost, now) to identify when a model's dated pricing card may change, allowing callers to safely reuse earlier builds until that time.
  • Added the selector-revision-suffix catalog axis for Google Vertex, so google-vertex/claude-opus-4-8 resolves exactly to claude-opus-4-8@default.

Changed

  • Reduced the bundled model catalog size and loading overhead.
  • Updated role defaults so Factory Droid image models are preferred for the image role, allowing generate_image to work with only a Factory login.
  • Updated smol and slow role defaults to use the currently bundled Cerebras and OpenAI Codex model identifiers.

Fixed

  • Fixed Mistral reasoning models so streamed thinking appears in the transcript and is preserved for subsequent turns.
  • Fixed later-turn HTTP 422 errors in Mistral-hosted DeepSeek conversations when assistant reasoning history is included.
  • Fixed DeepSeek V4.1 Flash image attachments being dropped for publisher-namespaced model IDs such as ds/deepseek-flash.

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Breaking Changes

  • Custom tool loaders now resolve the injected host SDK themselves; constructors take cwd as their first argument instead of a pi namespace.
  • WebMCP now uses Chrome's native WebMCP support through WebMcpController, which accepts a Puppeteer Page. The former page-hook installation helpers, bridge constant, and page-hook types have been removed; Tern tabs no longer support WebMCP.
  • Provider-qualified model selectors in --model, settings, roles, and task agents must identify an exact model ID or alias. Near matches now fail with a helpful error and suggestions instead of silently selecting a sibling model. Bare selectors such as opus retain fuzzy matching, and the Google Vertex Claude alias remains supported. Older migrated web fallback chains replace the retired Codex entry with openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol.

Added

  • Agents can send an interim response without ending the current task by writing Markdown to out:// (or using > out:// in bash), with streaming output up to 64 KiB. This is enabled by default in the interactive TUI and can be configured with out.enabled; it is disabled by default in print, RPC, and ACP sessions.
  • Added computer-tool approval modes: tools.approval["computer.control"] can allow unattended foreground control on dedicated machines or deny the request without prompting. The approval mode does not itself grant access.
  • Added optional task-file input (task.file=true), allowing subagent instructions to be loaded from an absolute UTF-8 file path or local:// URL with the submitted path and SHA-256 recorded (#17133 by @iliaal).
  • Sessions can be pinned or unpinned from the resume picker with Alt+K and Alt+Shift+K, or the remappable app.session.pin and app.session.unpin actions. Pinned sessions move to the top and take priority in search results (#9837 by @zhang17-24).
  • Added PI_LINK_SCHEME and optional PI_LINK_HOST, allowing file links from SSH or tmux sessions to open through a desktop protocol handler (#15855 by @KunoLu).
  • Extensions and RPC clients can start live voice sessions using their own WebRTC peer, including phone-browser clients, through ctx.startLive({ sdpOffer }) or live_connect (#17013 by @materemias).
  • Extensions can customize assistant-text rendering and rerender individual replies through the opt-in extensionRenderers.assistantText setting (#16238 by @materemias), and can register interactive link schemes with extension callbacks and renderer fallbacks (#16239 by @materemias).
  • /debug reports now include session-logs.txt containing all log records written by the reporting session and its subagents for the current day.
  • Added prewalk.from and prewalk.into settings to choose the planning model and handoff target (default @default → @smol); /prewalk [into] and /prewalk restart [from] [into] override them for one command (#17373 by @H4vC).

Changed

  • Improved startup responsiveness by deferring nonessential UI, accessibility, browser, provider, slash-command, and interactive-composer loading until needed, and by overlapping independent session preparation work.
  • OpenAI live steering is now disabled by default for GPT models. Typed messages wait for the next tool boundary unless providers.openaiLiveSteering is enabled, avoiding unsupported in-flight message errors.
  • HTML exports now match the /stats visual design with a higher-contrast light theme, timeline and full-trace views, and navigation for nested subagents and advisors. Packaged viewers use integrity-pinned shared assets and require network access; source checkouts continue to embed the viewer.
  • tui.autoGraph now defaults to auto: charts are refined by the judge for supported native TypeSafe judge models and otherwise use the built-in estimate.
  • Filesystem changes are detected automatically, so manual scan-cache invalidation after edits is no longer needed.
  • Model refreshes use substantially less CPU by reusing unchanged models and avoiding repeated model-cache database opens.
  • Overlapping sloppy-edit operations are now applied sequentially, with each operation matched against the result of the preceding operation.
  • PI_INTENT_PROMPT and PI_INTENT_DESC can now customize the system guidance and tool-schema description for the i intent field.
  • Printed JSON values in eval, including large Python dictionaries and lists, are displayed as collapsible JSON trees.
  • Codex and Claude rate-limit reset warnings now appear once per reset across conversations and windows, only during the final six hours; the status line continues to show the countdown.
  • The . and c continue shortcuts now simply answer an unanswered last message instead of adding a hidden continue directive.
  • With bash.gitGuard enabled, checkout, switch, restore, reset, and stash operations run safely in linked worktrees, while discarded uncommitted changes are preserved for recovery.
  • Sessions with computer use enabled receive the desktop guide before the first computer call.
  • The Tern interface now presents warnings, errors, update notices, file reads, and completed background work consistently with chat messages.
  • Muted live voice sessions remain visibly muted while the voice model starts work.

Fixed

  • Fixed live configuration reloads missing edits immediately after a symlink switches to another file in the same directory.
  • Fixed auth-gateway model routing after catalog refreshes so bare model IDs select the highest-priority credentialed provider, with wire-format compatibility used as a tie-breaker.
  • Fixed chart recommendations that incorrectly inferred whether higher or lower values were better from row or column names; smart charting now uses the judge and avoids charting unsuitable numeric text such as page or step numbers.
  • Fixed binary data flowing through background Bash pipelines being displayed as corrupted text or counted as huge line output.
  • Fixed browser-tab closing delays when reattaching after a worker exits.
  • Fixed startup warnings being obscured by the Tern interface; they now appear as normal in-app warnings.
  • Fixed todo init clearing existing flat items when list was empty.
  • Fixed legacy extensions using detached event handlers failing to load.
  • Fixed MCP tools connected during vibe mode being activated prematurely and exceeding provider tool limits.
  • Fixed Bash services rejecting port 0 when a log readiness condition was also provided.
  • Fixed context usage and automatic compaction overstating token usage for inline tool descriptors.
  • Fixed terminal titles not being restored after exit on terminals without title-stack support, including Windows consoles.
  • Fixed cached approval directory grants that did not cover every target in a request.
  • Fixed text typed during startup being delayed until startup completed, including resumed drafts.
  • Fixed pull-request metadata and diffs remaining stale after a successful git push.
  • Fixed live voice playback clicks when audio stalls mid-word.
  • Fixed read rejecting artifact URLs from truncation hints under cached approvals or restricted folders.
  • Fixed peer task acknowledgements being mistaken for final answers, so wait continues until the peer replies.
  • Failed Hindsight memory recalls and retention operations now produce visible warnings instead of silently continuing without memory.
  • Fixed long conflict-path handling, conflict-content selectors, and stale conflict IDs after aborted merges or external file changes.
  • Fixed cancelled MCP OAuth flows and authentication lookups from crashing the session.
  • Fixed progress HUD entries for idle local-model workers that were no longer used.
  • Improved LSP diagnostics reliability across cold starts, push-diagnostics servers, save-only servers, retries, multi-server and multi-file edits, and reopened files; incomplete results are no longer reported as clean, and repeated clangd checks are faster.
  • Fixed live job widgets showing incidental text from files, web pages, or search results instead of the actual job status.
  • Fixed repeatedly failing advisor batches being retried indefinitely; they are now dropped after three failures.
  • Fixed thinking overrides on prefix-bound Claude models from losing their provider prefix behavior during compaction and retries.
  • Fixed failed large-value JSON queries from displaying the rejected value instead of a useful error.
  • Fixed F5 retry reporting no retry when a background result arrived after cancellation or interruption.
  • Fixed WebMCP invocation errors being empty and timed-out page invocations continuing to run.
  • Fixed HTTP 413 errors for Factory Droid GPT and Grok after computer-use sessions by removing obsolete screenshots from requests.
  • Fixed unavailable configured default or dictation models being silently replaced; omp now warns and identifies the fallback model.
  • Fixed LSP references for files in sibling Git worktrees by adding those checkouts to the active workspace.
  • Improved macOS computer-use behavior for applications without windows by opening the app and inspecting its returned window in one operation.

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Breaking Changes

  • Removed the invalidateFsScanCache API and the associated filesystem scan cache environment variables (FS_SCAN_CACHE_TTL_MS, FS_SCAN_EMPTY_RECHECK_MS, FS_SCAN_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES, and FS_SCAN_CACHE_MAX_BYTES); filesystem listing caches are now managed automatically.

Added

  • Added a built-in rsync implementation to the bash tool, compatible with rsync 3.5.1 and protocol versions 29–33 across Linux, macOS, and Windows. It supports local copies, SSH remote shells via -e, and rsync:// daemons without requiring an installed binary; daemon serving is not supported, and PI_DISABLE_UUTILS_DESTRUCTIVE continues to use the system rsync.
  • Added built-in ast-grep (also available as sg) support to the bash tool for structural code search and rewriting, including pattern matching, rewrites, context, headings, and JSON output.

Changed

  • Improved native filesystem search performance, including faster globbing and more efficient grep, fuzzy finding, workspace listing, and structural search/edit operations, while preserving deterministic result ordering and streaming behavior.
  • Native search and the bash tool's built-in rg, grep, fd, find, and ast-grep now stop filesystem work promptly when cancelled or timed out, including searches over JavaScript-provided filesystems and searches interrupted while reading large files.
  • The bash tool's built-in rg and recursive grep now search files in parallel while retaining path-ordered output; grep can also stop promptly after reaching maxCount.
  • Enabled safe reuse of unchanged directory listings when cache: true, with timestamp validation to prevent stale results.

Fixed

  • Fixed cancelled native directory searches leaving filesystem-provider requests running during startup.
  • Fixed built-in bash commands such as cat, grep, head, wc, read, and mapfile hanging at the end of FIFO input on macOS; read -t now returns at EOF.
  • Fixed ANSI text wrapping for styled underlines, background colours, and colon-form RGB colours.
  • Fixed clicking in native audio playback when the queue runs dry mid-waveform by smoothly fading output to silence and back.

@oh-my-pi/omp-stats

Changed

  • Improved omp stats session synchronization performance by approximately 2–5× on large histories. The first sync after upgrading may take up to a minute while the stats database is optimized.

Fixed

  • Fixed /stats and omp stats failing when another dashboard was syncing sessions or the configured port was already in use; busy dashboards are now reused, and a free port is selected when necessary.

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Breaking Changes

  • Moved PINNED_HUD_TOGGLE_ID to the coding agent's task output manager so reserving subagent IDs no longer loads the interactive editor.
  • Updated StatusLineHost.resetExpiryNotice to receive the session and manage warning deduplication; custom status-line components must use the new signature.
  • Removed ToolExecutionHandle.setToolActivityVisible; tool-activity visibility is now controlled by transcript components, while reply blocks remain visible by default.
  • Removed the @oh-my-pi/pi-tui/theme/defaults export. Bundled palettes are now available through getBuiltinThemes() from theme/defaults.json.

Fixed

  • Fixed table-chart direction detection so measures are not incorrectly marked as better based on header words; single-measure tables are now sent for judging when local charting is appropriate.
  • Fixed LSP diagnostics that incorrectly showed “No issues” before a language server had reported; such files are now shown as unverified. (#17293 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • Fixed Esc-Esc rewind in Tern selecting interrupted turns with no visible output instead of the last displayed turn.
  • Fixed streamed code blocks losing lines that scrolled off screen before the closing fence arrived; completed lines now remain available in scrollback. (#17333 by @gzkGit777)

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Added

  • Added logger.withContext(fields, fn) to attach contextual fields to all records produced during a function and its asynchronous work, plus logger.withoutContext(fn) for work that should not inherit caller context.

Fixed

  • Fixed untilAborted so promises passed to an already-aborted signal are still observed, preventing later rejections from being reported as unhandled.

@oh-my-pi/pi-wire

Added

  • Added the TSP json kind for displaying raw JSON values as interactive, expandable trees in the terminal.
  • Added TspEditorProps.typeahead support so programs that cannot yet read input can preserve typed text, display it at the caret, and submit it when input becomes available.

coreyhaines31/marketingskills ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.13.10v2.13.10 — ai-seo (2.11.0 → 2.12.0): agent readiness adds a skill file for your product: a public S… Stable2026-10-29T17:22:34Z
  • ai-seo (2.11.0 → 2.12.0): agent readiness adds a skill file for your product: a public SKILL.md that uses the context the buyer's agent already has to propose setup choices, quotes published pricing, and returns a prefilled checkout or signup URL (price recomputed on the server, parameters validated, agent orders tagged). Agent-only offers are fine when stated as information; forced upsells and instructions tuned to always push an offer are called out as prompt injection. Prompted by Ilias Ism's post on LinkDR's SKILL.md.
  • video (2.3.0 → 2.4.0): new reference code-rendered product films: every frame a pure function of time over real DOM snapshots of a seeded app, a private video repo with one worktree per video, claims checked against source with docs-vs-UI discrepancies logged, a running AGENTS.md of taste rules, multi-take TTS with a judge, beat-grid music cuts and loudness targets, contact-sheet review (judge models as hints only), and hero video measurement by video_id. Adapted from Elie Steinbock's Inbox Zero write-up.
  • ab-testing (2.0.3 → 2.0.4): opt-in elements such as a hero video underpower tests on overall conversion; use a triggered analysis or compare versions on viewer metrics.
  • Tools: SavvyCal is now listed as MCP-enabled (official remote server with OAuth), and its v1 API guide and CLI are corrected against the OpenAPI spec (#918, contributed by Derrick Reimer and Jesse Leite of SavvyCal).

Skill ZIP assets

Complete source catalog: 52 skills. Claude web target: 52 skills with reviewed short descriptions. Perplexity Computer target: 52 skills.

Choose <name>-claude-web.zip for the documented single-folder web upload format. Choose <name>-perplexity.zip for Perplexity Computer (SKILL.md at the archive root). Source ZIPs retain original metadata; they are not substitutes for omitted web ZIPs. Live client upload, account limits, and invocation behavior have not been verified.

Web omissions (also recorded in claude-web-manifest.json):

  • None.

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v15.6.0 Stable2026-10-29T17:01:29Z

My Work: one store for the viewer's state (#5187)

My Work used to keep its state in three places: a hand-rolled module store, component state in App, and React contexts. It now runs on one Zustand store split into four slices: work, view, rules and focus.

What you'll notice

  • Reload and Back/Forward work. The page, focus area and open request drawer are in the URL (?view=, ?focus=, ?request=).
  • Your list view, filter and sort are remembered between visits. The theme still follows your system or ?theme=. If browser storage is blocked, preferences last for the current visit only.
  • Live updates are steadier. A stopped source can't publish. An older read never overwrites a newer live model. When a live update overtakes a save, you see the current model and your save is kept. Heartbeats re-render only the status indicators.
  • The drawer follows its request by id and closes when the request leaves the feed. If the first read fails, you get a Retry button. A selected Closed filter stays visible even when nothing matches.
  • My Rules: one save at a time, with controls disabled while a save runs. Each Approve/Reject receipt is tied to the version the worker saved, and Undo is refused once the rule has changed since.
  • My Focus: all screens share one save history.

Worker

  • Rule saves return savedRuleId.
  • "Load in Claude Code" publishes the change to other open tabs.
  • The feed signature includes rulesFile.

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.5.0...v15.6.0
